name: highload-gpu
description: Use when solving CUDA, GPU performance, GPU profiling, multi-GPU, or NVIDIA-library tasks.

# HighloadGpu

Router for CUDA code-writing and high-performance GPU engineering. Default to a single-GPU CUDA hot path first; use multi-GPU or search/decoding guidance only when the task calls for it.

## Pick The Sub-Skill

- CUDA kernels, memory layout, CUTLASS/CUB, graphs, native hot paths: `write-cuda-hot-paths`
- Beam search, top-k/top-p decoding, frontier expansion, candidate selection: `optimize-gpu-search-and-decoding`
- Multi-GPU scaling, rank ownership, peer access, NCCL, overlap: `design-multigpu-systems`
- Python/Rust/C++/CUDA ownership boundaries: `choose-stack-boundaries`
- GPU model, compute capability, CUDA/library docs: `use-nvidia-docs`
- Timeline/stream/NCCL/launch gaps: `use-nsight-systems`
- One-kernel bottleneck: `use-nsight-compute`
- Illegal access/race/init/sync bug: `use-compute-sanitizer`
- Batch/concurrency/shard sweeps: `benchmark-and-sweep-gpu-configs`
- Kaggle/Molab/remote GPU profiling: `profile-remote-gpu-runs`

## Defaults

- Python: wrappers, notebooks, dashboards, CLI/torchrun launch.
- Rust: safe config, validation, artifact/log tooling, host services.
- C++ + CUDA: CPU/GPU hot paths, kernels, CUDA Graphs, NCCL, CUTLASS, CUB/CCCL.

Check current NVIDIA docs before hardware-specific advice. Require correctness checks and profiling evidence before architecture changes.


```

---
name: write-cuda-hot-paths
description: Use when writing or optimizing CUDA kernels, memory layouts, and GPU hot paths.
---

# Write CUDA Hot Paths

Primary skill for CUDA code-writing. Hot paths are fixed-capacity, allocation-free, profiler-driven, and explicit about layout, alignment, ownership, and synchronization.

## Workflow

1. State shapes, precision, target GPU, correctness oracle, and measured bottleneck.
2. Choose library vs custom kernel; use custom CUDA where layout, fusion, or control flow makes it worthwhile.
3. Design layout first, implement a small deterministic path, then profile.

## Rules

- Allocate device memory, library temp, NCCL buffers, and graph resources before repeated loops.
- Use explicit capacity guards; fail loudly on overflow.
- Keep CPU readback out of the steady-state path.
- Use CUDA Graphs for repeated launch templates; keep dynamic dispatch on host.
- Add static asserts for cross-kernel metadata size/alignment.
- Store only downstream-needed data; avoid materializing large intermediates when compact keys, indices, or reductions are enough.

## Library Defaults

CUTLASS/cuBLAS for GEMM/tensor-core kernels; CUB/CCCL for sort/reduce/scan/select; NCCL for GPU exchange/collectives; CUDA Runtime/Driver APIs for memory, streams, events, graphs, devices, and peer access.

## Avoid

Dynamic `cudaMalloc`, Python data-plane loops, fine-grained atomics when partition/sort/scan works, custom sort before checking CUB/CCCL, and synchronization that hides ownership bugs.


```

# Stream1 Piece-Transformer Kaggle 2xT4 Benchmark v9 QKV Fused - 2026-07-03

## Source

- Kaggle kernel: `trydotatwo/cayley-beam-transformer-2xt4-benchmark`, version 9.
- Branch: `codex/stream1-piece-transformer`.
- Commit: `83c95ea`.
- Hardware: Kaggle `NvidiaTeslaT4`, two Tesla T4 GPUs.
- Backend: native Stream1 piece-transformer fp16 path.
- Change under test: QKV projection fused as CUTLASS GEMM + bias, on top of the previous FF1 GEMM + bias + SiLU fusion.

Downloaded outputs:

- `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v9_qkv_fused_2026-07-03/stream1_transformer_benchmark_rows.csv`
- `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v9_qkv_fused_2026-07-03/cayley-beam-transformer-2xt4-benchmark.log`
- `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v9_qkv_fused_2026-07-03/stream1_transformer_benchmark_reports/`

## Best Points

| version | change | gpu | best b_micro | concurrency | rows/group | ms/group | candidates/s | scratch bytes |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 7 | clean tensor attention baseline | 0 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 120.4175 | 408179.9 | 655458304 |
| 7 | clean tensor attention baseline | 1 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 121.1607 | 405676.1 | 655458304 |
| 8 | FF1 fused | 0 | 512 | 2 | 1024 | 55.0670 | 446292.4 | 327729152 |
| 8 | FF1 fused | 1 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 109.5028 | 448865.3 | 655458304 |
| 9 | FF1 + QKV fused | 0 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 103.6711 | 474114.7 | 655458304 |
| 9 | FF1 + QKV fused | 1 | 512 | 2 | 1024 | 52.5856 | 467352.5 | 327729152 |

## Interpretation

- v9 vs v8:
  - GPU0: `446292.4 -> 474114.7` candidates/s (`1.062x`).
  - GPU1: `448865.3 -> 467352.5` candidates/s (`1.041x`).
- v9 vs v7 baseline:
  - GPU0: `408179.9 -> 474114.7` candidates/s (`1.162x`).
  - GPU1: `405676.1 -> 467352.5` candidates/s (`1.152x`).
- v9 remains below PyTorch transformer reference:
  - PyTorch direct forward best: `734786.8` candidates/s.
  - PyTorch `batch_process`: `630697.0` candidates/s.
- The QKV bias epilogue is a real improvement, but the remaining gap is still in the transformer attention/layout/remaining epilogues rather than FF1/QKV bias alone.

## Status

- Kaggle log reports branch `codex/stream1-piece-transformer` and commit `83c95ea`.
- Both GPU benchmark reports ended with `status=pass`.
- Bounded grep over the Kaggle log found no traceback/error/failure lines.

#include "stream1_transformer_fmha.hpp"

#include "config.hpp"
#include "cuda_check.hpp"

#include <cuda_bf16.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#if BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS
#include <cutlass/bfloat16.h>
#include <cutlass/numeric_types.h>
#if BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS_FMHA
#include "kernel_forward.h"
#endif
#endif

#include <stdexcept>

namespace beam {

constexpr std::uint32_t FMHA_SEQ51 = 51U;
constexpr std::uint32_t FMHA_DMODEL256 = 256U;
constexpr std::uint32_t FMHA_HEAD_DIM32 = 32U;
constexpr std::uint32_t FMHA_NHEAD8 = 8U;
constexpr std::uint32_t FMHA_QKV_STRIDE51 = 3U * FMHA_DMODEL256;

__device__ float stream1_fmha_load_scalar_device(const half* ptr, std::uint64_t idx, std::uint32_t dtype) {
    if (dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
        return __bfloat162float(reinterpret_cast<const __nv_bfloat16*>(ptr)[idx]);
    }
    return __half2float(ptr[idx]);
}

__device__ void stream1_fmha_store_scalar_device(half* ptr, std::uint64_t idx, float value, std::uint32_t dtype) {
    if (dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
        reinterpret_cast<__nv_bfloat16*>(ptr)[idx] = __float2bfloat16(value);
        return;
    }
    ptr[idx] = __float2half(value);
}

__global__ void stream1_transformer_fmha_pack_qkv_bmhk51_kernel(
    const half* __restrict__ qkv,
    half* __restrict__ packed_qkv,
    std::uint32_t dtype,
    std::uint32_t b_micro) {
    const std::uint64_t i = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    constexpr std::uint64_t matrix_values = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(FMHA_SEQ51) * FMHA_NHEAD8 * FMHA_HEAD_DIM32;
    const std::uint64_t total = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * 3ULL * matrix_values;
    if (i >= total) {
        return;
    }
    const std::uint64_t per_kind_values = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * matrix_values;
    const std::uint32_t kind = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(i / per_kind_values);
    const std::uint64_t local = i - static_cast<std::uint64_t>(kind) * per_kind_values;
    const std::uint32_t dim = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(local % FMHA_HEAD_DIM32);
    const std::uint32_t head = static_cast<std::uint32_t>((local / FMHA_HEAD_DIM32) % FMHA_NHEAD8);
    const std::uint32_t token = static_cast<std::uint32_t>((local / (FMHA_HEAD_DIM32 * FMHA_NHEAD8)) % FMHA_SEQ51);
    const std::uint32_t row = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(local / matrix_values);
    const std::uint64_t qkv_col = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(kind) * FMHA_DMODEL256 +
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(head) * FMHA_HEAD_DIM32 + dim;
    const std::uint64_t src_idx = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * FMHA_SEQ51 * FMHA_QKV_STRIDE51 +
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(token) * FMHA_QKV_STRIDE51 + qkv_col;
    const float value = stream1_fmha_load_scalar_device(qkv, src_idx, dtype);
    stream1_fmha_store_scalar_device(packed_qkv, i, value, dtype);
}

#if BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS && BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS_FMHA
template <typename Element, typename ArchTag>
void stream1_transformer_fmha_launch_typed(
    half* packed_qkv,
    half* context,
    std::uint32_t b_micro,
    cudaStream_t stream) {
    using Attention = AttentionKernel<
        Element,
        ArchTag,
        true,
        64,
        64,
        64,
        false,
        false>;
    constexpr std::uint64_t matrix_values = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(FMHA_SEQ51) * FMHA_NHEAD8 * FMHA_HEAD_DIM32;
    typename Attention::Params params{};
    params.query_ptr = reinterpret_cast<Element*>(packed_qkv);
    params.key_ptr = reinterpret_cast<Element*>(packed_qkv + matrix_values * b_micro);
    params.value_ptr = reinterpret_cast<Element*>(packed_qkv + 2ULL * matrix_values * b_micro);
    params.output_ptr = reinterpret_cast<Element*>(context);
    params.output_accum_ptr = nullptr;
    params.logsumexp_ptr = nullptr;
    params.scale = 0.1767766952966369f;
    params.num_heads = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_NHEAD8);
    params.num_batches = static_cast<int32_t>(b_micro);
    params.head_dim = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_HEAD_DIM32);
    params.head_dim_value = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_HEAD_DIM32);
    params.num_queries = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_SEQ51);
    params.num_keys = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_SEQ51);
    params.custom_mask_type = Attention::NoCustomMask;
    params.q_strideH = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_HEAD_DIM32);
    params.k_strideH = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_HEAD_DIM32);
    params.v_strideH = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_HEAD_DIM32);
    params.q_strideM = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_DMODEL256);
    params.k_strideM = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_DMODEL256);
    params.v_strideM = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_DMODEL256);
    params.q_strideB = static_cast<int64_t>(FMHA_SEQ51 * FMHA_DMODEL256);
    params.k_strideB = static_cast<int64_t>(FMHA_SEQ51 * FMHA_DMODEL256);
    params.v_strideB = static_cast<int64_t>(FMHA_SEQ51 * FMHA_DMODEL256);
    params.o_strideM = static_cast<int32_t>(FMHA_DMODEL256);
    constexpr auto kernel_fn = attention_kernel_batched_impl<Attention>;
    const int smem_bytes = static_cast<int>(sizeof(typename Attention::SharedStorage));
    if (smem_bytes > 0xc000) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(kernel_fn, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize, smem_bytes));
    }
    if (!Attention::check_supported(params)) {
        throw std::runtime_error("Stream1 piece_transformer CUTLASS FMHA does not support this tensor layout");
    }
    kernel_fn<<<params.getBlocksGrid(), params.getThreadsGrid(), smem_bytes, stream>>>(params);
}
#endif

void stream1_transformer_fmha_attention_cuda(
    half* qkv,
    half* packed_qkv,
    half* context,
    Stream1TransformerDims dims,
    std::uint32_t b_micro,
    cudaStream_t stream) {
    if (dims.seq_len != FMHA_SEQ51 || dims.d_model != FMHA_DMODEL256 ||
        dims.nhead != FMHA_NHEAD8 || dims.head_dim != FMHA_HEAD_DIM32) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer CUTLASS FMHA requires seq_len=51 d_model=256 nhead=8 head_dim=32");
    }
    if (qkv == nullptr || packed_qkv == nullptr || context == nullptr) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer CUTLASS FMHA requires qkv, packed_qkv, and context");
    }
#if BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS && BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS_FMHA
    const std::uint64_t qkv_total = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * FMHA_SEQ51 * FMHA_QKV_STRIDE51;
    stream1_transformer_fmha_pack_qkv_bmhk51_kernel<<<
        static_cast<unsigned>((qkv_total + 255ULL) / 256ULL),
        256,
        0,
        stream>>>(qkv, packed_qkv, dims.dtype, b_micro);
    if (dims.dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
        stream1_transformer_fmha_launch_typed<cutlass::bfloat16_t, cutlass::arch::Sm80>(packed_qkv, context, b_micro, stream);
        return;
    }
    if (dims.dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16) {
        stream1_transformer_fmha_launch_typed<cutlass::half_t, cutlass::arch::Sm80>(packed_qkv, context, b_micro, stream);
        return;
    }
    throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer CUTLASS FMHA dtype must be fp16 or bf16");
#else
    (void)qkv;
    (void)packed_qkv;
    (void)context;
    (void)dims;
    (void)b_micro;
    (void)stream;
    throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer CUTLASS FMHA requires CUTLASS example 41 headers");
#endif
}

} // namespace beam

#include "stream_benchmark_common.hpp"

namespace beam::bench {

struct Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources {
    std::vector<cudaStream_t> streams;
    stream1_weights::ScratchAllocation scratch{};
    std::vector<std::uint64_t*> parent_base;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t*> count;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t*> score;

    Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources() = default;
    Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources(const Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources&) = delete;
    Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources& operator=(const Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources&) = delete;

    ~Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources() {
        cleanup();
    }

    void create_streams_checked(std::uint32_t stream_count) {
        streams.reserve(stream_count);
        for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < stream_count; ++i) {
            cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
            BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
            streams.push_back(stream);
        }
    }

    void allocate(
        const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
        std::uint32_t b_micro,
        std::uint32_t concurrent) {
        scratch = stream1_weights::alloc_stream1_scratch(model, b_micro, concurrent);
        parent_base.assign(concurrent, nullptr);
        count.assign(concurrent, nullptr);
        score.assign(concurrent, nullptr);
        for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < concurrent; ++i) {
            parent_base[i] = device_alloc<std::uint64_t>(1);
            count[i] = device_alloc<std::uint32_t>(1);
            score[i] = device_alloc<std::uint32_t>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * MOVE_COUNT);
        }
    }

    void cleanup() noexcept {
        cudaDeviceSynchronize();
        for (std::uint64_t* ptr : parent_base) {
            cudaFree(ptr);
        }
        for (std::uint32_t* ptr : count) {
            cudaFree(ptr);
        }
        for (std::uint32_t* ptr : score) {
            cudaFree(ptr);
        }
        parent_base.clear();
        count.clear();
        score.clear();
        stream1_weights::free_stream1_scratch(scratch);
        destroy_streams(streams);
    }
};

std::vector<Stream1Result> benchmark_stream1_transformer(
    const stream1_weights::DeviceWeights& weights,
    const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
    const State128* states,
    std::uint32_t max_states,
    std::ofstream& report) {
    if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
        throw std::runtime_error("benchmark_stream1_transformer requires piece_transformer backend");
    }
    std::vector<Stream1Result> results;
    stream1_weights::TransformerNetworkViewHolder view_holder =
        stream1_weights::transformer_network_view(weights.transformer, model);

    const char* only_b_micro_env = std::getenv("BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_B_MICRO");
    const char* only_concurrency_env = std::getenv("BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_CONCURRENCY");
    const std::uint32_t only_b_micro = only_b_micro_env != nullptr && only_b_micro_env[0] != '\0'
        ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(parse_u64(only_b_micro_env, "BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_B_MICRO"))
        : 0U;
    const std::uint32_t only_concurrency = only_concurrency_env != nullptr && only_concurrency_env[0] != '\0'
        ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(parse_u64(only_concurrency_env, "BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_CONCURRENCY"))
        : 0U;

    report << "## Stream1 Piece Transformer\n\n";
    if (only_b_micro != 0U || only_concurrency != 0U) {
        report << "- filter_b_micro=" << only_b_micro << "\n";
        report << "- filter_concurrency=" << only_concurrency << "\n\n";
    }
    report << "| b_micro | concurrency | rows_per_launch_group | ms_per_launch_group | parents_per_sec | candidates_per_sec | scratch_bytes |\n";
    report << "|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n";
    for (std::uint32_t b_micro : TRANSFORMER_B_MICRO_SWEEP) {
        for (std::uint32_t concurrent : TRANSFORMER_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY_SWEEP) {
            if ((only_b_micro != 0U && b_micro != only_b_micro) ||
                (only_concurrency != 0U && concurrent != only_concurrency)) {
                continue;
            }
            const std::uint64_t rows_per_launch_group = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * concurrent;
            if (rows_per_launch_group > max_states) {
                report << "|" << b_micro << "|" << concurrent << "|" << rows_per_launch_group
                       << "|skip|skip|skip|0: exceeds prepared state batch|\n";
                std::cout << "stream1_transformer_micro_skip"
                          << " b_micro=" << b_micro
                          << " concurrency=" << concurrent
                          << " reason=exceeds_prepared_state_batch\n";
                continue;
            }

            const std::uint64_t scratch_bytes =
                stream1_weights::stream1_scratch_bytes(model, b_micro, concurrent);
            const std::uint64_t io_bytes =
                rows_per_launch_group *
                (sizeof(std::uint64_t) + sizeof(std::uint32_t) +
                 static_cast<std::uint64_t>(MOVE_COUNT) * sizeof(std::uint32_t));
            std::size_t free_bytes = 0;
            std::size_t total_bytes = 0;
            BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes));
            constexpr std::uint64_t safety_margin_bytes = 256ULL * 1024ULL * 1024ULL;
            if (scratch_bytes + io_bytes + safety_margin_bytes > free_bytes) {
                report << "|" << b_micro << "|" << concurrent << "|" << rows_per_launch_group
                       << "|skip|skip|skip|" << scratch_bytes
                       << ": estimated allocation exceeds available GPU memory"
                       << " free_bytes=" << free_bytes
                       << " io_bytes=" << io_bytes << "|\n";
                std::cout << "stream1_transformer_micro_skip"
                          << " b_micro=" << b_micro
                          << " concurrency=" << concurrent
                          << " scratch_bytes=" << scratch_bytes
                          << " free_bytes=" << free_bytes
                          << " reason=estimated_allocation_exceeds_available_memory\n";
                continue;
            }

            Stream1TransformerBenchmarkResources resources;
            resources.create_streams_checked(concurrent);
            resources.allocate(model, b_micro, concurrent);
            std::vector<Stream1TransformerScratchView> scratch_views;
            scratch_views.reserve(concurrent);
            for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < concurrent; ++i) {
                scratch_views.push_back(stream1_weights::transformer_scratch_view(resources.scratch, model, b_micro, i));
                const std::uint64_t base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(i) * b_micro;
                BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(resources.parent_base[i], &base, sizeof(base), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
                BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(resources.count[i], &b_micro, sizeof(b_micro), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
            }

            const std::uint32_t iterations = b_micro >= 4096 ? 4U : 6U;
            const float ms = time_gpu_ms(resources.streams, iterations, [&]() {
                for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < concurrent; ++i) {
                    stream1_transformer_inference_cuda(
                        states,
                        resources.parent_base[i],
                        resources.count[i],
                        view_holder.view,
                        scratch_views[i],
                        resources.score[i],
                        b_micro,
                        resources.streams[i]);
                }
            });
            const double parents = static_cast<double>(rows_per_launch_group);
            const double parent_per_sec = parents * 1000.0 / static_cast<double>(ms);
            const double candidate_per_sec = parents * static_cast<double>(MOVE_COUNT) * 1000.0 / static_cast<double>(ms);
            results.push_back(Stream1Result{b_micro, concurrent, ms, parent_per_sec, candidate_per_sec});
            report << "|" << b_micro
                   << "|" << concurrent
                   << "|" << rows_per_launch_group
                   << "|" << std::fixed << std::setprecision(4) << ms
                   << "|" << std::setprecision(1) << parent_per_sec
                   << "|" << candidate_per_sec
                   << "|" << scratch_bytes << "|\n";
            std::cout << "stream1_transformer_micro"
                      << " b_micro=" << b_micro
                      << " concurrency=" << concurrent
                      << " rows_per_launch_group=" << rows_per_launch_group
                      << " ms_per_launch_group=" << std::fixed << std::setprecision(4) << ms
                      << " parents_per_sec=" << std::setprecision(1) << parent_per_sec
                      << " candidates_per_sec=" << candidate_per_sec
                      << " scratch_bytes=" << scratch_bytes
                      << "\n";
        }
    }
    report << "\n";
    return results;
}


} // namespace beam::bench

  802: void stream1_transformer_qk_batched_launch(
  803:     const half* qkv,

  841: void stream1_transformer_attention_launch(
  842:     half* qkv,
  843:     const Stream1TransformerScratchView& scratch,
  844:     Stream1TransformerDims dims,
  845:     std::uint32_t b_micro,

  848:     if (dims.seq_len != STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_SEQ51 ||
  849:         dims.d_model != STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_DMODEL256 ||
  850:         dims.nhead != STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_NHEAD8 ||
  851:         dims.head_dim != STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_DIM32) {
  852:         throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer tensor attention requires seq_len=51 d_model=256 nhead=8 head_dim=32");
  853:     }
  854:     if (qkv == nullptr || scratch.attention_scores_probs == nullptr ||
  855:         scratch.attention_context == nullptr) {
  856:         throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer tensor attention requires qkv, score scratch, and attention_context");
  857:     }

  859:     if (attention_backend == Stream1TransformerAttentionBackend::Sm80Bf16Fmha) {

  861:             qkv,

  871:         static_cast<unsigned>(((static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_NHEAD8 *
  872:             STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_HEAD_OUTPUT_STRIDE51) + 255ULL) / 256ULL),
  873:         256,
  874:         0,
  875:         stream>>>(qkv, scratch.attention_scores_probs, dims.dtype, b_micro);
  876:     stream1_transformer_softmax51_kernel<<<
  877:         dim3(b_micro, STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_NHEAD8),
  878:         256,
  879:         0,

  881:     stream1_transformer_pv_batched_launch(

  898: __global__ void stream1_transformer_cls_layernorm_kernel(
  899:     const half* __restrict__ tokens,
  900:     half* __restrict__ cls,
  901:     const half* __restrict__ gamma,
  902:     const half* __restrict__ beta,
  903:     Stream1TransformerDims dims,
  904:     std::uint32_t b_micro) {
  905:     const std::uint32_t row = blockIdx.x;
  906:     if (row >= b_micro) {
  907:         return;
  908:     }
  909:     extern __shared__ float reduce[];
  910:     const std::uint64_t in_base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len * dims.d_model;
  911:     const std::uint64_t out_base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.d_model;
  912:     float sum = 0.0f;
  913:     for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < dims.d_model; col += blockDim.x) {
  914:         sum += stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(tokens, in_base + col, dims.dtype);
  915:     }
  916:     reduce[threadIdx.x] = sum;
  917:     __syncthreads();
  918:     for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
  919:         if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
  920:             reduce[threadIdx.x] += reduce[threadIdx.x + stride];
  921:         }
  922:         __syncthreads();
  923:     }
  924:     const float mean = reduce[0] / static_cast<float>(dims.d_model);
  925:     float var_sum = 0.0f;
  926:     for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < dims.d_model; col += blockDim.x) {
  927:         const float x = stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(tokens, in_base + col, dims.dtype);
  928:         const float d = x - mean;
  929:         var_sum += d * d;
  930:     }
  931:     reduce[threadIdx.x] = var_sum;
  932:     __syncthreads();
  933:     for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
  934:         if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
  935:             reduce[threadIdx.x] += reduce[threadIdx.x + stride];
  936:         }
  937:         __syncthreads();
  938:     }
  939:     const float inv_std = rsqrtf(reduce[0] / static_cast<float>(dims.d_model) + 1.0e-5f);
  940:     for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < dims.d_model; col += blockDim.x) {
  941:         const float x = stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(tokens, in_base + col, dims.dtype);
  942:         const float y = (x - mean) * inv_std *
  943:             stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(gamma, col, dims.dtype) +
  944:             stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(beta, col, dims.dtype);
  945:         stream1_transformer_store_scalar_device(cls, out_base + col, y, dims.dtype);
  946:     }
  947: }

  949: __global__ void stream1_transformer_score_quantize_kernel(
  950:     const half* __restrict__ logits,
  951:     const half* __restrict__ output_bias,
  952:     const std::uint32_t* __restrict__ count,
  953:     std::uint32_t* __restrict__ score_ring,
  954:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  955:     Stream1TransformerDims dims) {
  956:     const std::uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
  957:     const std::uint32_t total = b_micro * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
  958:     if (i >= total) {
  959:         return;
  960:     }
  961:     const std::uint32_t row = i / static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
  962:     const std::uint32_t move = i % static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
  963:     if (row >= *count) {
  964:         return;
  965:     }
  966:     const float q = stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(logits, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.output_dim + move, dims.dtype) +
  967:         stream1_transformer_load_scalar_device(output_bias, move, dims.dtype);
  968:     score_ring[i] = stream1_transformer_score_key_from_float_device(q);
  969: }

  971: void stream1_transformer_inference_cuda(
  972:     const State128* current_frontier_states,
  973:     const std::uint64_t* parent_base,
  974:     const std::uint32_t* count,

  976:     const Stream1TransformerScratchView& scratch,

  994:     }

 1001:     const std::uint32_t token_rows = b_micro * dims.seq_len;

 1044:         stream1_transformer_attention_launch(

# Stream1 Piece Transformer PyTorch Gap Analysis - 2026-07-02

## Question

Explain why the native Stream1 piece-transformer backend is slower than the
PyTorch fast path on Kaggle T4, even though the native MLP backend is faster
than its PyTorch equivalent.

## Evidence

### PyTorch model path

The Kaggle artifact `vladkuznetsov266/megaminx-qtransformer-1782210824`
uses the model code under:

- `test_results/kaggle_model_artifact_download/megaminx-transformer/pilgrim/model.py`

The fast transformer block does:

1. `LayerNorm`
2. one `F.linear(..., in_proj_weight, in_proj_bias)` for QKV
3. reshape Q, K, V to `[batch, heads, seq, head_dim]`
4. one `F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, dropout_p=0.0, is_causal=False)`
5. one output `F.linear`
6. residual add
7. `LayerNorm`
8. `Linear -> SiLU -> Linear`
9. residual add

The piece input fast path precomputes:

- `fast_slot_projected`
- `fast_piece_static`
- `fast_piece_masks`

So the PyTorch speed is not from a different model. It is the same folded
piece-token construction idea plus PyTorch's optimized SDPA and linear kernels.

PyTorch documents `scaled_dot_product_attention` as an efficient implementation
which can automatically select optimized CUDA kernels, including FlashAttention
and memory-efficient attention, when the input supports them:

- https://docs.pytorch.org/docs/2.12/generated/torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention.html
- https://docs.pytorch.org/docs/2.12/backends.html

### Native Stream1 path

The current native code in `cuda/stream1.cu` still decomposes attention:

1. `stream1_transformer_add_qkv_bias_kernel`
2. `stream1_transformer_qk_batched_launch`
   - loops over 8 heads
   - launches CUTLASS `GemmBatched` per head
3. `stream1_transformer_pack_v51_kernel`
4. `stream1_transformer_softmax51_kernel`
5. `stream1_transformer_pv_batched_launch`
   - loops over 8 heads
   - launches CUTLASS `GemmBatched` per head

That is at least 19 launches per transformer layer for attention alone:

- 1 QKV-bias kernel
- 8 QK GEMM launches
- 1 V-pack kernel
- 1 softmax kernel
- 8 PV GEMM launches

Then each layer also has separate launches for:

- LayerNorm copies
- QKV linear
- attention output linear
- residual + bias
- FF1 linear
- bias + SiLU
- FF2 linear
- residual + bias

For 4 transformer layers this creates a launch-heavy and scratch-heavy path.

### Scratch traffic

At the Kaggle native best point `B_MICRO=512, concurrency=2`, the launch group
contains 1024 parents. Native scratch is:

| buffer | bytes | MiB |
|---|---:|---:|
| tokens | 26,738,688 | 25.5 |
| qkv | 80,216,064 | 76.5 |
| attention_scores_probs | 87,031,808 | 83.0 |
| attention_context | 26,738,688 | 25.5 |
| ff_hidden | 106,954,752 | 102.0 |
| logits | 49,152 | 0.047 |
| total | 327,729,152 | 312.547 |

At 2048 parents, the same native layout would reserve about 625.094 MiB.
PyTorch direct forward at batch 2048 measured 0.872 GiB peak on Kaggle T4,
but most importantly its SDPA path does not require our QK/prob/V/context
roundtrip as many independent kernels.

## Measurements

### Transformer, Kaggle 2xT4

From `test_results/transformer_inference_example_kaggle_speed_2026-07-01.md`:

- PyTorch direct forward best: `734,786.8 candidates/s` on one T4 at batch 2048.
- PyTorch `batch_process`: `630,697.0 candidates/s` for 65,536 rows.

From `test_results/stream1_transformer_kaggle_benchmark_v5_2026-07-01.md`:

- Native Stream1 GPU0: `406,086.6 candidates/s`.
- Native Stream1 GPU1: `415,118.8 candidates/s`.
- Best native point: `B_MICRO=512, concurrency=2`.

Native is:

- `0.55x-0.57x` of PyTorch direct forward.
- `0.64x-0.66x` of PyTorch `batch_process`.

### MLP control, Kaggle 2xT4

From `test_results/stream1_mlp_native_vs_torch_kaggle_v2_2026-07-01.md`:

- Native CUTLASS MLP aggregate: `32,144,707.0 candidates/s`.
- PyTorch preindexed MLP aggregate: `14,897,439.9 candidates/s`.
- Native / PyTorch aggregate: `2.1577x`.

This rules out "native CUDA is slower than PyTorch" as the general cause.
The slowdown is specific to the transformer native backend.

## Root Cause

The native transformer backend is slower because its attention is not equivalent
to PyTorch's fused SDPA execution strategy.

PyTorch pays for one high-level SDPA call per layer and lets CUDA backend
dispatch pick a fused attention implementation when possible. Our native path
materializes and reloads intermediate attention tensors across many kernels:

- QKV bias is separate from QKV GEMM.
- QK is launched once per head.
- softmax is a separate global-memory pass.
- V is repacked into scratch.
- PV is launched once per head.
- residual/bias and SiLU epilogues are separate kernels.

The current native implementation uses tensor cores for QK/PV GEMMs, but it
still loses on launch count, global-memory roundtrips, and per-head
fragmentation. On T4 this overhead dominates enough that PyTorch is faster.

## What To Do Next

Do not add a PyTorch fallback. The native transformer backend needs a better
attention backend.

Priority order:

1. Capture a bounded PyTorch profiler or Nsight trace on Kaggle T4 for exactly
   one warmed direct-forward batch, to record actual SDPA kernel names.
2. Add a bounded capture mode to `stream_benchmark` so Nsight profiles one
   `B_MICRO=512, concurrency=2` group instead of the broad sweep.
3. Replace per-head QK + softmax + V-pack + per-head PV with a native fused
   short-SDPA backend for fixed `seq_len=51`, `head_dim=32`, `nhead=8`.
4. Fuse epilogues where possible:
   - QKV GEMM + bias
   - FF1 GEMM + bias + SiLU
   - attention output GEMM + residual + bias
   - FF2 GEMM + residual + bias
5. Keep the MLP path separate and unchanged.

## Current Confidence

High. The code-path comparison and Kaggle measurements agree. The missing
piece is exact PyTorch CUDA kernel names on Kaggle T4, but the root cause does
not depend on those names: PyTorch uses a single SDPA abstraction, while native
currently decomposes attention into many launch and scratch stages.

# Stream1 Transformer CUTLASS FMHA Backend Split - 2026-07-02

## Scope

Implemented a separate Stream1 piece-transformer attention backend split:

- `fp16` stays on the T4/SM75-safe CUTLASS tensor QK -> softmax -> PV path.
- `bf16` on SM80+ routes to `cuda/stream1_transformer_fmha.cu`, which uses CUTLASS example 41 fixed-seqlen FMHA.
- No PyTorch fallback, no distillation, and no MLP path changes.
- CUTLASS FMHA headers are detected in CMake from `${CUTLASS_DIR}/examples/41_fused_multi_head_attention`; if unavailable, the bf16/SM80 FMHA path fails closed.

## Layout

The FMHA path uses the existing transformer attention scratch as packed Q/K/V storage:

- Source QKV GEMM output: `[row, seq, 3 * d_model]`.
- Packed FMHA input: `[Q all rows][K all rows][V all rows]`, each in BMHK physical layout.
- Output: FMHA writes directly into `attention_context` in `[row, seq, d_model]` layout.

The first FMHA attempt had a Q/K/V pack ordering bug (`[row][Q,K,V]` instead of `[Q rows][K rows][V rows]`); the reference test caught this with `max_abs_score_key_error=14011`. The corrected pack layout passes the reference test.

## Verification

Docker image: `gpu-dev-cutlass-nsight:2026-05-24`.

Commands run:

```bash
cmake --build build-stream1-opt --target stream1_transformer_cuda_tests contract_tests dispatcher_cuda_tests stream_benchmark -j2
./build-stream1-opt/stream1_transformer_cuda_tests
./build-stream1-opt/contract_tests
./build-stream1-opt/dispatcher_cuda_tests
```

Results:

- `stream1_transformer_cuda_tests=pass`
- `contract_tests=pass`
- `dispatcher_cuda_tests=pass`

Smoke runs on local `NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3070 Laptop GPU, compute capability 8.6`:

- fp16 staged path, `512x2`: `216536.5` candidates/sec, `327729152` scratch bytes.
- bf16 converted fixture, FMHA path, `512x1`: `278827.7` candidates/sec, `163864576` scratch bytes.
- rerun after the final backend split, bf16 converted fixture, FMHA path, `512x1`: `287829.6` candidates/sec, `163864576` scratch bytes.
- fp16 staged path after moving transformer executor to `cuda/stream1_transformer.cu`, `512x1`: `239614.2` candidates/sec, `163864576` scratch bytes.
- post-split smoke after moving transformer executor to `cuda/stream1_transformer.cu`, bf16 converted fixture, FMHA path, `512x1`: `271579.2` candidates/sec, `163864576` scratch bytes.

The local GPU was observed at `86C`, `P3`, and `33W / 55W`; these numbers are smoke evidence only and should not be treated as an A100/T4 performance gate.

## Notes

CUTLASS example-41 FMHA is available and wired, but local fp16 FMHA testing on SM86 was slower than the clean tensor path for this short `seq_len=51, head_dim=32` workload. Therefore fp16 remains on the known T4-safe tensor backend, while bf16/SM80+ uses the separate FMHA backend requested for modern cards.

## 2026-07-03 Residual Epilogue Update

After splitting the transformer executor, the attention-output projection and FF2 projection now use a transformer-local CUTLASS residual GEMM path (`D = A * B + residual`) followed by a narrow bias-add kernel. This removes the previous large intermediate branch materialization for those two residual projections while keeping MLP Stream1 untouched.

Verification after the update:

- `stream1_transformer_cuda_tests=pass`
- `contract_tests=pass`
- `dispatcher_cuda_tests=pass`
- fp16 staged path, `512x1`: `575995.4` candidates/sec, `163864576` scratch bytes.
- bf16 converted fixture with FMHA path, `512x1`: `675808.8` candidates/sec, `163864576` scratch bytes.

These are still local smoke numbers, not a T4/A100 performance gate.
## Kaggle 2xT4 v7 Check - 2026-07-03

- Kaggle kernel: `trydotatwo/cayley-beam-transformer-2xt4-benchmark`, version 7.
- GitHub ref cloned in notebook: `codex/stream1-piece-transformer`.
- GitHub commit in Kaggle log: `fd1f131`.
- Status: `COMPLETE`, per-GPU reports `status=pass`.
- T4 backend: `stream1_backend=piece_transformer`, `stream1_gemm=TensorOp_Sm75_common_for_T4_and_RTX3070`.
- Best GPU0: `b_micro=1024`, `concurrency=2`, `ms_per_launch_group=120.4175`, `candidates_per_sec=408179.9`, `scratch_bytes=655458304`.
- Best GPU1: `b_micro=1024`, `concurrency=2`, `ms_per_launch_group=121.1607`, `candidates_per_sec=405676.1`, `scratch_bytes=655458304`.
- Output saved under `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v7_2026-07-03/`.

## Rejected fp16 CUTLASS FMHA Experiment - 2026-07-03

Tried routing fp16 attention through CUTLASS example-41 FMHA with `cutlass::arch::Sm75` as an explicit T4-style backend. Local SM86 smoke passed correctness, but speed collapsed to `148613.9 candidates/sec` at `b_micro=512`, `concurrency=1`, compared with the staged tensor path's prior `575995.4 candidates/sec` local smoke. The experiment was reverted; current committed fp16/T4 path remains the staged tensor QK/softmax/PV backend.
## Nsight Source-Path Profile - 2026-07-03

Profile command saved `test_results/nsys_stream1_transformer_source_sm86_512x1_2026-07-03.nsys-rep` and `.sqlite` for the current staged tensor fp16 path. Under Nsight overhead the microbenchmark reported `87813.1 candidates/sec`, so absolute speed is not comparable to non-profiled smoke, but relative kernel time is useful:

- CUTLASS GEMMs: ~45.2% of GPU kernel time.
- `stream1_transformer_layernorm256_copy_kernel`: ~14.0%.
- `stream1_transformer_bias_silu_kernel`: ~10.7%.
- `stream1_transformer_add_qkv_bias_kernel`: ~7.3%.
- `stream1_transformer_softmax51_kernel`: ~5.7%.
- `stream1_transformer_pack_v51_kernel`: ~4.1%.
- QK/PV `GemmBatched` attention kernels together: ~7.2%.

Conclusion: replacing only QK/PV attention with a FlashAttention-style kernel is not enough on this shape; the larger targets are FF/GEMM epilogues, LayerNorm, and elementwise bias/activation kernels.

## Rejected fp16 Warp Attention Experiment - 2026-07-03

Tried a custom fixed-shape warp attention kernel for fp16 `seq_len=51`, `nhead=8`, `head_dim=32` that adds QKV bias on read and writes context directly. Existing `stream1_transformer_cuda_tests` passed, but local SM86 throughput collapsed to `46881.4 candidates/sec` at `b_micro=512`, `concurrency=1`. This was reverted. The result confirms that a scalar warp-softmax kernel without tensor-core QK/PV work is not a viable T4 backend.

# Stream1 Piece-Transformer QKV Bias Fused CUTLASS 2026-07-03

## Change

- Added a transformer-only QKV `linear+bias` CUTLASS path in `cuda/stream1_transformer.cu`.
- The attention QKV projection now writes pre-biased QKV through `GemmUniversalWithBroadcast` and `LinearCombinationBiasElementwise` with identity activation.
- Removed the old separate QKV bias kernel from the transformer path.
- Updated the SM80 BF16 FMHA pack path to consume pre-biased QKV, so it does not add QKV bias a second time.
- The MLP Stream1 path remains unchanged.
- No fallback or distillation path was added.

# Stream1 Piece Transformer Clean Tensor Attention - 2026-07-01

## Change

Rewrote the Stream1 piece-transformer attention path away from the previous pointer-array `GemmArray` implementation.

Current attention layout:

- QKV projection stays as the existing CUTLASS linear GEMM.
- QKV bias is applied in-place to the QKV scratch.
- QK uses CUTLASS `GemmBatched` per head over a fixed padded score/prob scratch.
- Softmax uses the fixed `seq_len=51` warp kernel over the score rows.
- V is packed into the same attention scratch for tensor-op alignment.
- PV uses CUTLASS `GemmBatched` per head.
- Removed transformer pointer-table scratch (`attention_q_ptrs`, `attention_k_ptrs`, score/prob/context pointer arrays); MLP Stream1 path is unchanged.

This is not a fallback path and does not change model weights or outputs.

## Verification

Docker image: `gpu-dev-cutlass-nsight:2026-05-24`.

```bash
docker run --rm --gpus all -v "${PWD}:/workspace" -w /workspace gpu-dev-cutlass-nsight:2026-05-24 bash -lc "cmake --build build-stream1-opt --target stream1_transformer_cuda_tests stream_benchmark -j2 && ./build-stream1-opt/stream1_transformer_cuda_tests"
```

```text

```

For comparison, the previous local `512x1` GemmArray report in this worktree showed `565930.9` candidates/s with `164126720` scratch bytes, so this clean tensor path is slightly faster locally and uses less scratch in that point.

## Notes

A first scalar warp-only rewrite was tested and rejected because it passed correctness but dropped to `51780.6` candidates/s at `512x1`. The final implementation keeps tensor-core QK/PV while removing the pointer-array attention machinery.

# Stream1 Transformer Kaggle 2xT4 Benchmark v4 - 2026-07-01

Kernel: `trydotatwo/cayley-beam-transformer-2xt4-benchmark`
Version: 4
Status: `KernelWorkerStatus.COMPLETE`
Branch commit tested: `08d1ddd` (`Rewrite Stream1 transformer attention with tensor GEMM`)

## Best Rows

| GPU | B_MICRO | concurrency | rows/group | ms/group | parents/s | candidates/s | scratch bytes |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 0 | 1024 | 1 | 1024 | 61.1542 | 16744.6 | 401869.4 | 328253440 |
| 1 | 512 | 2 | 1024 | 59.7437 | 17139.9 | 411356.9 | 328253440 |

## Aggregate

| version | aggregate parents/s | aggregate candidates/s |
|---|---:|---:|
| v3 warp attention | 19520.0 | 468478.3 |
| v4 tensor GEMM attention | 33884.5 | 813226.3 |

## Comparison To PyTorch Notebook Fast Path

The public `vladkuznetsov266/transformer-inference-example` fast PyTorch path measured on Kaggle T4:

- Direct model forward best: `734786.8` candidates/s at `batch=2048`.
- Searcher-like `batch_process`: `630697.0` candidates/s for `65536` rows with `eval_batch_size=16384`.

The current native CUDA Stream1 piece-transformer backend is now about:

- `401869.4 / 734786.8 = 0.55x` of best direct PyTorch forward on GPU0.
- `411356.9 / 734786.8 = 0.56x` of best direct PyTorch forward on GPU1.
- `401869.4 / 630697.0 = 0.64x` of PyTorch `batch_process` on GPU0.
- `411356.9 / 630697.0 = 0.65x` of PyTorch `batch_process` on GPU1.

## Interpretation

This is not a hard performance ceiling. The tensor-GEMM attention rewrite moved Kaggle T4 throughput from about `232k-236k` candidates/s per T4 in v3 to about `402k-411k` candidates/s per T4 in v4, but the same checkpoint's PyTorch fast path is still `1.5x-1.8x` faster depending on comparison mode.

The remaining likely bottlenecks are not the QK/PV tensor attention itself. Local Nsight after the rewrite showed most time in transformer block linear GEMMs and unfused elementwise kernels: LayerNorm materialization, bias+SiLU, QKV bias, residual+bias add, softmax, V packing, and final quantization.

Next plausible optimization work:

1. Move FFN `bias + SiLU` into the first FFN GEMM epilogue.
2. Move attention-out and FFN2 `bias + residual add` into GEMM epilogues.
3. Write QKV GEMM output directly in the layout needed by QK/PV and apply QKV bias there, removing the separate QKV-bias and V-pack kernels.
4. Fuse the fixed `seq_len=51` softmax and PV staging more tightly, or use one persistent attention kernel if it beats CUTLASS `GemmArray` launch overhead on T4.
5. Keep MLP path separate; these are transformer-only changes.

Target: matching PyTorch `batch_process` (`~0.63M` candidates/s per T4) is realistic. Matching or slightly exceeding direct PyTorch forward (`~0.73M` candidates/s per T4) may require deeper epilogue fusion and fewer kernel launches.

inline void publish_threshold_for_benchmark(StaticDeviceMemory& memory, std::uint32_t threshold) {
    const std::uint32_t thresholds[2]{threshold, threshold};
    const std::uint32_t initialized[2]{1U, 1U};
    const std::uint32_t active = 0;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
        memory.streams.current_threshold,
        thresholds,
        sizeof(thresholds),
        cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
        memory.streams.threshold_initialized,
        initialized,
        sizeof(initialized),
        cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
        memory.streams.current_threshold_active_index,
        &active,
        sizeof(active),
        cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
}

struct BenchmarkThresholdBuffers {
    std::uint32_t* current_threshold = nullptr;
    std::uint32_t* threshold_initialized = nullptr;
    std::uint32_t* active_index = nullptr;
};

inline BenchmarkThresholdBuffers alloc_benchmark_threshold_buffers() {
    BenchmarkThresholdBuffers buffers;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&buffers.current_threshold, 2ULL * sizeof(std::uint32_t)));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&buffers.threshold_initialized, 2ULL * sizeof(std::uint32_t)));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&buffers.active_index, sizeof(std::uint32_t)));
    return buffers;
}

inline void attach_benchmark_threshold_buffers(StaticDeviceMemory& memory, const BenchmarkThresholdBuffers& buffers) {
    memory.streams.current_threshold = buffers.current_threshold;
    memory.streams.threshold_initialized = buffers.threshold_initialized;
    memory.streams.current_threshold_active_index = buffers.active_index;
}

inline void free_benchmark_threshold_buffers(BenchmarkThresholdBuffers& buffers) {
    cudaFree(buffers.current_threshold);
    cudaFree(buffers.threshold_initialized);
    cudaFree(buffers.active_index);
    buffers = {};
}

inline std::uint64_t parse_u64(const char* text, const char* name) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (end == text || *end != '\0') {
        throw std::invalid_argument(std::string("invalid numeric argument: ") + name);
    }
    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(value);
}

inline std::uint32_t parse_next_u32(const std::string& text, std::size_t& pos, const char* context) {
    while (pos < text.size() && (text[pos] < '0' || text[pos] > '9')) {
        ++pos;
    }
    if (pos >= text.size()) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("missing integer in ") + context);
    }
    std::uint64_t value = 0;
    while (pos < text.size() && text[pos] >= '0' && text[pos] <= '9') {
        value = value * 10ULL + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(text[pos] - '0');
        if (value > std::numeric_limits<std::uint8_t>::max()) {
            throw std::runtime_error(std::string("integer out of uint8 range in ") + context);
        }
        ++pos;
    }
    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value);
}

inline std::string read_text_file(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
    if (!file) {
        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required text file: " + path.string());
    }
    return std::string(std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(file), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
}

inline std::vector<std::uint8_t> load_p900_generators(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    const std::string text = read_text_file(path);
    std::size_t pos = text.find("\"actions\"");
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("p900 generator json missing actions");
    }
    pos = text.find('[', pos);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("p900 generator json malformed actions");
    }
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> generators(MOVE_COUNT * STATE_STORAGE_LEN);
    for (std::uint32_t move = 0; move < MOVE_COUNT; ++move) {
        pos = text.find('[', pos + 1);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            throw std::runtime_error("p900 generator json missing move array");
        }
        for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_LEN; ++p) {
            generators[move * STATE_STORAGE_LEN + p] =
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(parse_next_u32(text, pos, "p900 generator"));
        }
        for (std::uint32_t p = STATE_LEN; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
            generators[move * STATE_STORAGE_LEN + p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(p);
        }
    }
    return generators;
}

inline State128 load_central_state(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    const std::string text = read_text_file(path);
    std::size_t pos = text.find("\"central_state\"");
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("puzzle info json missing central_state");
    }
    pos = text.find('[', pos);
    if (pos == std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error("puzzle info json malformed central_state");
    }
    State128 state{};
    for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_LEN; ++p) {
        state.v[p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(parse_next_u32(text, pos, "central_state"));
    }
    for (std::uint32_t p = STATE_LEN; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
        state.v[p] = 0;
    }
    return state;
}

inline State128 load_initial_state_from_test_csv(const std::filesystem::path& path, std::uint64_t puzzle_id) {
    std::ifstream file(path);
    if (!file) {
        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required csv file: " + path.string());
    }
    std::string line;
    std::getline(file, line);
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        const std::size_t comma = line.find(',');
        if (comma == std::string::npos) {
            continue;
        }
        const std::uint64_t row_id = parse_u64(line.substr(0, comma).c_str(), "initial_state_id");
        if (row_id != puzzle_id) {
            continue;
        }
        const std::size_t first_quote = line.find('"', comma);
        const std::size_t last_quote = line.rfind('"');
        if (first_quote == std::string::npos || last_quote == std::string::npos || last_quote <= first_quote) {
            throw std::runtime_error("test csv malformed initial_state row");
        }
        const std::string state_text = line.substr(first_quote + 1, last_quote - first_quote - 1);
        std::size_t pos = 0;
        State128 state{};
        for (std::uint32_t p = 0; p < STATE_LEN; ++p) {
            state.v[p] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(parse_next_u32(state_text, pos, "initial_state"));
        }
        for (std::uint32_t p = STATE_LEN; p < STATE_STORAGE_LEN; ++p) {
            state.v[p] = 0;
        }
        return state;
    }
    throw std::runtime_error("requested puzzle_id not found in test csv");
}

inline void require_aligned(const void* ptr, std::uintptr_t alignment, const char* name) {
    if (reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(ptr) % alignment != 0) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("device pointer alignment failed: ") + name);
    }
}

template <typename T>
T* device_alloc(std::uint64_t count) {
    T* ptr = nullptr;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&ptr, count * sizeof(T)));
    return ptr;
}

struct Timer {
    cudaStream_t control = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
    std::vector<cudaEvent_t> done;

    explicit Timer(std::uint32_t stream_count) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&control, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
        done.resize(stream_count);
        for (cudaEvent_t& event : done) {
            BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event));
        }
    }

    ~Timer() {
        for (cudaEvent_t event : done) {
            cudaEventDestroy(event);
        }
        cudaEventDestroy(start);
        cudaEventDestroy(stop);
        cudaStreamDestroy(control);
    }
};

template <typename Fn>
float time_gpu_ms(const std::vector<cudaStream_t>& streams, std::uint32_t iterations, Fn launch) {
    Timer timer(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(streams.size()));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    launch();
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(timer.start, timer.control));
    for (cudaStream_t stream : streams) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(stream, timer.start, 0));
    }
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < iterations; ++i) {
        launch();
    }
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < streams.size(); ++i) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(timer.done[i], streams[i]));
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(timer.control, timer.done[i], 0));
    }
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(timer.stop, timer.control));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(timer.stop));
    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, timer.start, timer.stop));
    return elapsed_ms / static_cast<float>(iterations);
}

template <typename Fn>
float time_single_stream_ms(cudaStream_t stream, Fn launch) {
    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
    launch();
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop));
    cudaEventDestroy(start);
    cudaEventDestroy(stop);
    return elapsed_ms;
}

inline std::vector<cudaStream_t> create_streams(std::uint32_t count) {
    std::vector<cudaStream_t> streams(count);
    for (cudaStream_t& stream : streams) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    }
    return streams;
}

inline void destroy_streams(std::vector<cudaStream_t>& streams) {
    for (cudaStream_t stream : streams) {
        cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
    }
    streams.clear();
}

struct Stream1Result {
    std::uint32_t b_micro = 0;
    std::uint32_t concurrent = 0;
    float ms = 0.0f;
    double parent_per_sec = 0.0;
    double candidate_per_sec = 0.0;
};

struct StreamResult {
    std::string name;
    std::uint32_t param_a = 0;
    std::uint32_t param_b = 0;
    float ms = 0.0f;
    double candidate_per_sec = 0.0;
};

inline std::vector<State128> make_state_batch(
    State128 seed,
    std::uint32_t count,
    std::uint32_t num_classes) {
    std::vector<State128> states(count, seed);
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        states[i].v[0] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((states[i].v[0] + i) % num_classes);
        states[i].v[STATE_LEN] = 0;
    }
    return states;
}

std::vector<Stream1Result> benchmark_stream1_mlp(
    const stream1_weights::DeviceWeights& weights,
    const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
    const State128* states,
    const std::uint8_t* generators,
    std::uint32_t max_states,
    std::ofstream& report);

std::vector<Stream1Result> benchmark_stream1_transformer(
    const stream1_weights::DeviceWeights& weights,
    const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
    const State128* states,
    std::uint32_t max_states,
    std::ofstream& report);

std::vector<StreamResult> benchmark_stream2(
    const State128* states,
    const std::uint8_t* generators,
    const State128* central,
    const Hash128* zobrist,
    std::ofstream& report);

std::vector<StreamResult> benchmark_stream3(std::ofstream& report);

std::vector<StreamResult> benchmark_stream4(std::ofstream& report);

} // namespace beam::bench

  655:     alloc_and_copy(d.fast_piece_static, h.fast_piece_static, "fast_piece_static");
  656:     alloc_and_copy(d.cls_token, h.cls_token, "cls_token");

  658:     alloc_and_copy(d.input_ln_beta, h.input_ln_beta, "input_ln_beta");
  659:     alloc_and_copy(d.output_ln_gamma, h.output_ln_gamma, "output_ln_gamma");
  660:     alloc_and_copy(d.output_ln_beta, h.output_ln_beta, "output_ln_beta");
  661:     d.blocks.resize(h.blocks.size());

  664:         const HostTransformerBlockBytes& hb = h.blocks[i];
  665:         DeviceTransformerBlockWeights& db = d.blocks[i];
  666:         alloc_and_copy(db.ln1_gamma, hb.ln1_gamma, (prefix + "_ln1_gamma").c_str());
  667:         alloc_and_copy(db.ln1_beta, hb.ln1_beta, (prefix + "_ln1_beta").c_str());
  668:         alloc_and_copy(db.attn_qkv_weight, hb.attn_qkv_weight, (prefix + "_attn_qkv_weight").c_str());
  669:         alloc_and_copy(db.attn_qkv_bias, hb.attn_qkv_bias, (prefix + "_attn_qkv_bias").c_str());
  670:         alloc_and_copy(db.attn_out_weight, hb.attn_out_weight, (prefix + "_attn_out_weight").c_str());
  671:         alloc_and_copy(db.attn_out_bias, hb.attn_out_bias, (prefix + "_attn_out_bias").c_str());
  672:         alloc_and_copy(db.ln2_gamma, hb.ln2_gamma, (prefix + "_ln2_gamma").c_str());
  673:         alloc_and_copy(db.ln2_beta, hb.ln2_beta, (prefix + "_ln2_beta").c_str());
  674:         alloc_and_copy(db.ff1_weight, hb.ff1_weight, (prefix + "_ff1_weight").c_str());
  675:         alloc_and_copy(db.ff1_bias, hb.ff1_bias, (prefix + "_ff1_bias").c_str());
  676:         alloc_and_copy(db.ff2_weight, hb.ff2_weight, (prefix + "_ff2_weight").c_str());
  677:         alloc_and_copy(db.ff2_bias, hb.ff2_bias, (prefix + "_ff2_bias").c_str());
  678:     }
  679:     alloc_and_copy(d.output_weight, h.output_weight, "output_weight");
  680:     alloc_and_copy(d.output_bias, h.output_bias, "output_bias");
  681:     alloc_and_copy_typed(d.piece_positions, h.piece_positions, "piece_positions");
  682:     alloc_and_copy_typed(d.piece_mask, h.piece_mask, "piece_mask");
  683:     alloc_and_copy_typed(d.piece_types, h.piece_types, "piece_types");

  686: inline void free_weights(DeviceWeights& device);

  688: inline DeviceWeights upload_weights(const HostWeightBytes& host) {
  689:     DeviceWeights device;
  690:     try {
  691:         if (host.model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  692:             upload_transformer_weights(host, device);
  693:             return device;
  694:         }
  695:         if (host.model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  696:             throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in upload_weights");
  697:         }
  698:         alloc_and_copy(device.input_weight, host.input_weight, "input_weight");
  699:         alloc_and_copy(device.input_bias, host.input_bias, "input_bias");
  700:         if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  701:             alloc_and_copy(device.input_ln_gamma, host.input_ln_gamma, "input_ln_gamma");
  702:             alloc_and_copy(device.input_ln_beta, host.input_ln_beta, "input_ln_beta");
  703:         }
  704:         alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_weight, host.hidden_weight, "hidden_weight");
  705:         alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_bias, host.hidden_bias, "hidden_bias");
  706:         if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  707:             alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_ln_gamma, host.hidden_ln_gamma, "hidden_ln_gamma");
  708:             alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_ln_beta, host.hidden_ln_beta, "hidden_ln_beta");
  709:         }
  710:         device.residual_fc1_weight.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  711:         device.residual_fc1_bias.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  712:         device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  713:         device.residual_fc1_ln_beta.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  714:         device.residual_fc2_weight.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  715:         device.residual_fc2_bias.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  716:         device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  717:         device.residual_fc2_ln_beta.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  718:         for (std::uint32_t block = 0; block < host.model.residual_count; ++block) {
  719:             const std::string prefix = "residual" + std::to_string(block);
  720:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_weight[block], host.residual_fc1_weight[block], (prefix + "_fc1_weight").c_str());
  721:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_bias[block], host.residual_fc1_bias[block], (prefix + "_fc1_bias").c_str());
  722:             if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  723:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma[block], host.residual_fc1_ln_gamma[block], (prefix + "_fc1_ln_gamma").c_str());
  724:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_ln_beta[block], host.residual_fc1_ln_beta[block], (prefix + "_fc1_ln_beta").c_str());
  725:             }
  726:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_weight[block], host.residual_fc2_weight[block], (prefix + "_fc2_weight").c_str());
  727:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_bias[block], host.residual_fc2_bias[block], (prefix + "_fc2_bias").c_str());
  728:             if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  729:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma[block], host.residual_fc2_ln_gamma[block], (prefix + "_fc2_ln_gamma").c_str());
  730:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_ln_beta[block], host.residual_fc2_ln_beta[block], (prefix + "_fc2_ln_beta").c_str());
  731:             }
  732:         }
  733:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_weight_table, device.residual_fc1_weight, "residual_fc1_weight_table");
  734:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_bias_table, device.residual_fc1_bias, "residual_fc1_bias_table");
  735:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_weight_table, device.residual_fc2_weight, "residual_fc2_weight_table");
  736:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_bias_table, device.residual_fc2_bias, "residual_fc2_bias_table");
  737:         if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  738:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma_table, device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma, "residual_fc1_ln_gamma_table");
  739:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_ln_beta_table, device.residual_fc1_ln_beta, "residual_fc1_ln_beta_table");
  740:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma_table, device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma, "residual_fc2_ln_gamma_table");
  741:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_ln_beta_table, device.residual_fc2_ln_beta, "residual_fc2_ln_beta_table");
  742:         }
  743:         alloc_and_copy(device.output_weight, host.output_weight, "output_weight");
  744:         alloc_and_copy(device.output_bias, host.output_bias, "output_bias");
  745:         return device;
  746:     } catch (...) {
  747:         free_weights(device);
  748:         throw;
  749:     }
  750: }
  751: 
  752: inline void free_weights(DeviceWeights& device) {
  753:     cudaFree(device.input_weight);
  754:     cudaFree(device.input_bias);
  755:     cudaFree(device.input_ln_gamma);
  756:     cudaFree(device.input_ln_beta);
  757:     cudaFree(device.hidden_weight);
  758:     cudaFree(device.hidden_bias);
  759:     cudaFree(device.hidden_ln_gamma);
  760:     cudaFree(device.hidden_ln_beta);
  761:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_weight) {
  762:         cudaFree(ptr);
  763:     }
  764:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_bias) {
  765:         cudaFree(ptr);
  766:     }
  767:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma) {
  768:         cudaFree(ptr);
  769:     }
  770:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_ln_beta) {
  771:         cudaFree(ptr);
  772:     }
  773:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_weight) {
  774:         cudaFree(ptr);
  775:     }
  776:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_bias) {
  777:         cudaFree(ptr);
  778:     }
  779:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma) {
  780:         cudaFree(ptr);
  781:     }
  782:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_ln_beta) {
  783:         cudaFree(ptr);
  784:     }
  785:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_weight_table);
  786:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_bias_table);
  787:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma_table);
  788:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_ln_beta_table);
  789:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc2_weight_table);
  790:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc2_bias_table);
  791:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma_table);
  792:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc2_ln_beta_table);
  793:     cudaFree(device.output_weight);
  794:     cudaFree(device.output_bias);
  795:     DeviceTransformerWeights& t = device.transformer;
  796:     cudaFree(t.fast_slot_projected);
  797:     cudaFree(t.fast_piece_static);
  798:     cudaFree(t.cls_token);
  799:     cudaFree(t.input_ln_gamma);
  800:     cudaFree(t.input_ln_beta);
  801:     cudaFree(t.output_ln_gamma);
  802:     cudaFree(t.output_ln_beta);
  803:     for (DeviceTransformerBlockWeights& b : t.blocks) {
  804:         cudaFree(b.ln1_gamma);
  805:         cudaFree(b.ln1_beta);
  806:         cudaFree(b.attn_qkv_weight);
  807:         cudaFree(b.attn_qkv_bias);
  808:         cudaFree(b.attn_out_weight);
  809:         cudaFree(b.attn_out_bias);
  810:         cudaFree(b.ln2_gamma);
  811:         cudaFree(b.ln2_beta);
  812:         cudaFree(b.ff1_weight);
  813:         cudaFree(b.ff1_bias);
  814:         cudaFree(b.ff2_weight);
  815:         cudaFree(b.ff2_bias);
  816:     }
  817:     cudaFree(t.output_weight);
  818:     cudaFree(t.output_bias);
  819:     cudaFree(t.piece_positions);
  820:     cudaFree(t.piece_mask);
  821:     cudaFree(t.piece_types);
  822:     device = DeviceWeights{};
  823: }
  824: 
  825: inline void free_stream1_scratch(ScratchAllocation& scratch);
  826: 
  827: inline ScratchAllocation alloc_stream1_scratch(
  828:     const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
  829:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  830:     std::uint32_t lane_count) {
  831:     ScratchAllocation scratch;
  832:     try {
  833:         const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model) * lane_count;
  834:         if (model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  835:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.hidden1, rows * model.hidden1 * sizeof(half)));
  836:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.hidden2, rows * model.hidden2 * sizeof(half)));
  837:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.residual, rows * model.hidden2 * sizeof(half)));
  838:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.output, rows * model.output_dim * sizeof(half)));
  839:             return scratch;
  840:         }
  841:         if (model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  842:             const std::uint64_t token_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
  843:             const std::uint64_t qkv_values = rows * model.seq_len * 3ULL * model.d_model;
  844:             const std::uint64_t score_values = rows * model.nhead * transformer_attention_score_stride(model);
  845:             const std::uint64_t context_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
  846:             const std::uint64_t ff_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.ff_dim;
  847:             const std::uint64_t logits_values = rows * model.output_dim;
  848:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_tokens, token_values * sizeof(half)));
  849:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_qkv, qkv_values * sizeof(half)));
  850:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs, score_values * sizeof(half)));
  851:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_attention_context, context_values * sizeof(half)));
  852:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_ff_hidden, ff_values * sizeof(half)));
  853:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_logits, logits_values * sizeof(half)));
  854:             return scratch;
  855:         }
  856:         throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in scratch allocation");
  857:     } catch (...) {
  858:         free_stream1_scratch(scratch);
  859:         throw;
  860:     }
  861: }
  862: inline void free_stream1_scratch(ScratchAllocation& scratch) {
  863:     cudaFree(scratch.hidden1);
  864:     cudaFree(scratch.hidden2);
  865:     cudaFree(scratch.residual);
  866:     cudaFree(scratch.output);
  867:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_tokens);
  868:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_qkv);
  869:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs);
  870:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_attention_context);
  871:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_ff_hidden);
  872:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_logits);
  873:     scratch = ScratchAllocation{};
  874: }
  875: 
  876: inline Stream1NetworkDims network_dims(const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
  877:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  878:         throw std::runtime_error("piece_transformer Stream1 inference is not implemented in the MLP network view");
  879:     }
  880:     return Stream1NetworkDims{
  881:         model.state_len,
  882:         model.num_classes,
  883:         model.hidden1,
  884:         model.hidden2,
  885:         model.residual_count,
  886:         model.output_dim,
  887:         model.dtype,
  888:         model.normalization};
  889: }
  890: 
  891: inline std::vector<const half*> const_pointer_vector(const std::vector<half*>& ptrs) {
  892:     return std::vector<const half*>(ptrs.begin(), ptrs.end());
  893: }
  894: inline Stream1TransformerDims transformer_dims(const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
  895:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  896:         throw std::runtime_error("MLP Stream1 model cannot be viewed as a piece_transformer");
  897:     }
  898:     return Stream1TransformerDims{
  899:         model.state_len,
  900:         model.num_classes,
  901:         model.num_pieces,
  902:         model.max_piece_size,
  903:         model.seq_len,
  904:         model.d_model,
  905:         model.nhead,
  906:         model.head_dim,
  907:         model.transformer_layers,
  908:         model.ff_dim,
  909:         model.output_dim,
  910:         model.dtype};
  911: }
  912: 
  913: struct TransformerNetworkViewHolder {
  914:     std::vector<Stream1TransformerBlockView> blocks;
  915:     Stream1TransformerNetworkView view{};
  916: };
  917: 
  918: inline TransformerNetworkViewHolder transformer_network_view(
  919:     const DeviceTransformerWeights& weights,
  920:     const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
  921:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  922:         throw std::runtime_error("MLP Stream1 weights cannot be viewed as a piece_transformer");
  923:     }
  924:     if (weights.blocks.size() != model.transformer_layers) {
  925:         throw std::runtime_error("device transformer block count does not match manifest");
  926:     }
  927:     TransformerNetworkViewHolder holder;
  928:     holder.blocks.resize(weights.blocks.size());
  929:     for (std::size_t i = 0; i < weights.blocks.size(); ++i) {
  930:         const DeviceTransformerBlockWeights& b = weights.blocks[i];
  931:         holder.blocks[i] = Stream1TransformerBlockView{
  932:             b.ln1_gamma,
  933:             b.ln1_beta,
  934:             b.attn_qkv_weight,
  935:             b.attn_qkv_bias,
  936:             b.attn_out_weight,
  937:             b.attn_out_bias,
  938:             b.ln2_gamma,
  939:             b.ln2_beta,
  940:             b.ff1_weight,
  941:             b.ff1_bias,
  942:             b.ff2_weight,
  943:             b.ff2_bias};
  944:     }
  945:     holder.view = Stream1TransformerNetworkView{
  946:         weights.fast_slot_projected,
  947:         weights.fast_piece_static,
  948:         weights.cls_token,
  949:         weights.input_ln_gamma,
  950:         weights.input_ln_beta,
  951:         weights.output_ln_gamma,
  952:         weights.output_ln_beta,
  953:         holder.blocks.data(),
  954:         weights.output_weight,
  955:         weights.output_bias,
  956:         weights.piece_positions,
  957:         weights.piece_mask,
  958:         weights.piece_types,
  959:         transformer_dims(model)};
  960:     return holder;
  961: }
  962: 
  963: inline void free_transformer_network_view(TransformerNetworkViewHolder& holder) {
  964:     holder = TransformerNetworkViewHolder{};
  965: }
  966: 
  967: inline Stream1CutlassScratch mlp_scratch_view(
  968:     const ScratchAllocation& scratch,
  969:     const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
  970:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  971:     std::uint32_t lane) {
  972:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  973:         throw std::runtime_error("piece_transformer Stream1 scratch cannot be viewed as MLP scratch");
  974:     }
  975:     const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model);
  976:     const std::uint64_t lane_rows = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(lane) * rows;
  977:     return Stream1CutlassScratch{
  978:         scratch.hidden1 + lane_rows * model.hidden1,
  979:         scratch.hidden2 + lane_rows * model.hidden2,
  980:         scratch.residual + lane_rows * model.hidden2,
  981:         scratch.output + lane_rows * model.output_dim};
  982: }
  983: 
  984: inline Stream1TransformerScratchView transformer_scratch_view(const ScratchAllocation& scratch) {
  985:     return Stream1TransformerScratchView{
  986:         scratch.transformer_tokens,
  987:         scratch.transformer_qkv,
  988:         scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs,
  989:         scratch.transformer_attention_context,
  990:         scratch.transformer_ff_hidden,
  991:         scratch.transformer_logits};
  992: }
  993: 
  994: inline Stream1TransformerScratchView transformer_scratch_view(
  995:     const ScratchAllocation& scratch,
  996:     const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
  997:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  998:     std::uint32_t lane) {
  999:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
 1000:         throw std::runtime_error("MLP Stream1 scratch cannot be viewed as a piece_transformer");
 1001:     }
 1002:     const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model);
 1003:     const std::uint64_t lane_rows = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(lane) * rows;
 1004:     return Stream1TransformerScratchView{
 1005:         scratch.transformer_tokens + lane_rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model,
 1006:         scratch.transformer_qkv + lane_rows * model.seq_len * 3ULL * model.d_model,
 1007:         scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs + lane_rows * model.nhead * transformer_attention_score_stride(model),
 1008:         scratch.transformer_attention_context + lane_rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model,
 1009:         scratch.transformer_ff_hidden + lane_rows * model.seq_len * model.ff_dim,
 1010:         scratch.transformer_logits + lane_rows * model.output_dim};
 1011: }
 1012: #endif

## 117. Codex

Time: `2026-07-03T08:54:31.929Z` · source line `170`

```text
The LayerNorm variants in `test_results` were consistently worse at the tested `512x1` point, and the residual+bias broadcast path also underperformed. I’m avoiding those as standalone recommendations; the remaining LayerNorm-related idea has to be fusion with an existing adjacent stage, not another isolated LN kernel.
```

> # Stream1 Piece-Transformer Kaggle 2xT4 Benchmark v9 QKV Fused - 2026-07-03

## Source

- Kaggle kernel: `trydotatwo/cayley-beam-transformer-2xt4-benchmark`, version 9.
- Branch: `codex/stream1-piece-transformer`.
- Commit: `83c95ea`.
- Hardware: Kaggle `NvidiaTeslaT4`, two Tesla T4 GPUs.
- Backend: native Stream1 piece-transformer fp16 path.
- Change under test: QKV projection fused as CUTLASS GEMM + bias, on top of the previous FF1 GEMM + bias + SiLU fusion.

Downloaded outputs:

- `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v9_qkv_fused_2026-07-03/stream1_transformer_benchmark_rows.csv`
- `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v9_qkv_fused_2026-07-03/cayley-beam-transformer-2xt4-benchmark.log`
- `test_results/kaggle_stream1_transformer_2xt4_v9_qkv_fused_2026-07-03/stream1_transformer_benchmark_reports/`

## Best Points

| version | change | gpu | best b_micro | concurrency | rows/group | ms/group | candidates/s | scratch bytes |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 7 | clean tensor attention baseline | 0 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 120.4175 | 408179.9 | 655458304 |
| 7 | clean tensor attention baseline | 1 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 121.1607 | 405676.1 | 655458304 |
| 8 | FF1 fused | 0 | 512 | 2 | 1024 | 55.0670 | 446292.4 | 327729152 |
| 8 | FF1 fused | 1 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 109.5028 | 448865.3 | 655458304 |
| 9 | FF1 + QKV fused | 0 | 1024 | 2 | 2048 | 103.6711 | 474114.7 | 655458304 |
| 9 | FF1 + QKV fused | 1 | 512 | 2 | 1024 | 52.5856 | 467352.5 | 327729152 |

## Interpretation

- v9 vs v8:
  - GPU0: `446292.4 -> 474114.7` candidates/s (`1.062x`).
  - GPU1: `448865.3 -> 467352.5` candidates/s (`1.041x`).
- v9 vs v7 baseline:
  - GPU0: `408179.9 -> 474114.7` candidates/s (`1.162x`).
  - GPU1: `405676.1 -> 467352.5` candidates/s (`1.152x`).
- v9 remains below PyTorch transformer reference:
  - PyTorch direct forward best: `734786.8` candidates/s.
  - PyTorch `batch_process`: `630697.0` candidates/s.
- The QKV bias epilogue is a real improvement, but the remaining gap is still in the transformer attention/layout/remai
ning epilogues rather than FF1/QKV bias alone.

## Status

- Kaggle log reports branch `codex/stream1-piece-transformer` and commit `83c95ea`.
- Both GPU benchmark reports ended with `status=pass`.
- Bounded grep over the Kaggle log found no traceback/error/failure lines.
